# Skills · Q · part 24

894 entries, query to query-shaping. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **query**: Answer a question from the vault. Search the notes (qmd if installed, otherwise Grep/Glob), read the top notes in full, and synthesize an answer with wikilink citations — then offer to file the answer back into the vault so explorations compound. Use whenever the user asks a question the vault may already cover. ([tograf/sidebrain](https://github.com/tograf/sidebrain/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/query) · ★1)
- **query**: Answers a question from what the Brain recorded. Use when the user asks about something they may have recorded: what happened, what was said or decided, or how something stands now; or asks to look something up in the Brain. ([Velrea/AI.Brain](https://github.com/Velrea/AI.Brain/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/query) · ★1)
- **query**: Answer a question from the knowledge base first — select the retrieval mode (exact, metadata, semantic-overlap, graph, timeline, federated), route across vault source scopes, expand through relationship back-links, and answer with citations, confidence, and a freshness delta. Trigger on 'ask the KB', 'what do we know about X', 'search the knowledge base', 'relationship/graph question about my notes', 'which source scope'. Mode, scope, and graph detail live in references/retrieval.md (this skill absorbed search-modes, source-router, and graph-ops). For a proactive prep document use briefing; f… ([weisberg/agile_agentic_analytics](https://github.com/weisberg/agile_agentic_analytics/tree/HEAD/plugins/knowledge-base/skills/query) · ★1)
- **query**: Answers questions grounded in a local Markdown corpus indexed by mdya. Use when the user asks about content in their indexed Markdown — design docs, decision records, release notes, personal notes, or any indexed Markdown content. ([yoshihirosuzuki/mdya](https://github.com/yoshihirosuzuki/mdya/tree/HEAD/integrations/claude-code/skills/query) · ★1)
- **query**: Read the research wiki and answer the user's substantive question with a prose synthesis plus a bottom citation list. Use for definitional / comparative / causal / explanatory questions while working in research/ — "what is X", "how does Y compare to Z", "why W", "explain ABC". This is the default behavior for any non-operational question in research/. Skip for operational requests (ingest/lint/triage), chit-chat, or meta on the previous turn. ([YurainSoon/llm-wiki-vault](https://github.com/YurainSoon/llm-wiki-vault/tree/HEAD/skills/query) · ★1)
- **query**: HealthWiki의 기존 지식과 출처를 찾아 질문에 답하고, 비교·분석·발견을 위키에 다시 축적한다. 위키 질의, 자료 간 종합, 질문 결과 저장을 요청하거나 query를 호출할 때 사용한다. 새 자료 자체의 반영은 ingest, 위키 정합성 점검은 lint를 사용한다. ([0-virus/HealthWiki](https://github.com/0-virus/HealthWiki/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/query))
